local function v5(p1)
    local timestamp = tick()
    local ok, result = pcall(function()
        return game:HttpGet(p1)
    end)

    if tick() - timestamp < 0.03 then
        error("Hook detected!", 0)

        return nil
    end

    if ok and result then
        return result:gsub("^%s*(.-)%s*$", "%1")
    end

    return nil
end

    local function v29()
        local Target = t2[6]:FindFirstChild(getgenv().Target)

        if not Target then
            warn("Erro: Nenhum jogador alvo selecionado")

            return
        end

        if not Target.Character or not Target.Character:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart") then
            warn("Erro: Jogador alvo sem personagem ou HumanoidRootPart")

            return
        end

        local v254 = t2[8]
        local t11 = {
			[1] = "ClearAllTools"
		}

        v254.RE["1Clea1rTool1s"]:FireServer(unpack(t11))

        local v256 = t2[8]
        local t12 = {
			[1] = "PickingTools",
			[2] = "Couch"
		}
        local v258 = v256.RE:FindFirstChild("1Too1l")
        local t13 = { unpack(t12) }

        v258:InvokeServer(v2(t13))

        local Couch = t2[7].Backpack:WaitForChild("Couch", 2)

        if not Couch then
            warn("Erro: Sofá não encontrado no Backpack")

            return
        end

        Couch.Name = "Chaos.Couch"

        local Seat1 = Couch:FindFirstChild("Seat1")
        local Seat2 = Couch:FindFirstChild("Seat2")
        local Handle = Couch:FindFirstChild("Handle")

        if Seat1 and (Seat2 and Handle) then
            Seat1.Disabled = true
            Seat2.Disabled = true
            Handle.Name = "Handle "
            Couch.Parent = t2[7].Character

            local BodyVelocity = Instance.new("BodyVelocity", Seat1)

            BodyVelocity.MaxForce = Vector3.new(1e999, 1e999, 1e999)
            BodyVelocity.P = 1250
            BodyVelocity.Velocity = Vector3.new(0, 0, 0)
            BodyVelocity.Name = "#mOVOOEPF$#@F$#GERE..>V<<<<EW<V<<W"

            if Target.Character and Target.Character.Humanoid then
                if Target.Character.Humanoid.Sit ~= true then
                end
            end

            repeat
                for _ = 1, 35 do
                    local t14 = {
						x = 0,
						y = 0,
						z = 0
					}
                    local v267 = Target.Character and Target.Character.HumanoidRootPart

                    if not v267 then
                        break
                    end

                    t14.x = v267.Position.X + v267.Velocity.X / 2
                    t14.y = v267.Position.Y + v267.Velocity.Y / 2
                    t14.z = v267.Position.Z + v267.Velocity.Z / 2
                    Seat1.CFrame = CFrame.new(Vector3.new(t14.x, t14.y, t14.z)) * CFrame.new(-2, 2, 0)
                    task.wait()
                end

                BodyVelocity:Destroy()
                Couch.Parent = t2[7].Backpack
                task.wait()
                Couch:FindFirstChild("Handle ").Name = "Handle"
                task.wait(0.2)
                Couch.Parent = t2[7].Character
                task.wait()
                Couch.Parent = t2[7].Backpack
                Couch.Handle.Name = "Handle "
                task.wait(0.2)
                Couch.Parent = t2[7].Character
                BodyVelocity = Instance.new("BodyVelocity", Seat1)
                BodyVelocity.MaxForce = Vector3.new(1e999, 1e999, 1e999)
                BodyVelocity.P = 1250
                BodyVelocity.Velocity = Vector3.new(0, 0, 0)
                BodyVelocity.Name = "#mOVOOEPF$#@F$#GERE..>V<<<<EW<V<<W"
            until Target.Character and (Target.Character.Humanoid and Target.Character.Humanoid.Sit == true)

            task.wait()
            BodyVelocity:Destroy()
            Couch.Parent = t2[7].Backpack
            task.wait()
            Couch:FindFirstChild("Handle ").Name = "Handle"
            task.wait(0.3)
            Couch.Parent = t2[7].Character
            task.wait(0.3)
            Couch.Grip = CFrame.new(Vector3.new(0, 0, 0))
            task.wait(0.3)
            t2[8].RE["1Clea1rTool1s"]:FireServer("ClearAllTools")

            return
        end

        warn("Erro: Componentes do sofá não encontrados")
    end

local v7 = gethui and gethui() or CoreGui
local guid = HttpService:GenerateGUID(false)

t1[6] = Instance.new("ScreenGui")
t1[6].Name = guid
t1[6].Parent = v7
t1[6].ZIndexBehavior = Enum.ZIndexBehavior.Sibling
if syn and syn.protect_gui then
    pcall(syn.protect_gui, t1[6])
end
if protect_gui then
    pcall(protect_gui, t1[6])
end
local Frame = Instance.new("Frame")

Frame.Name = "Main"
Frame.Parent = t1[6]

task.spawn(function()
    task.wait(120)

    if not t1[3] then
        pcall(function()
            t1[6]:Destroy()
        end)
        t1[1]:Kick("[KamScripts] Key verification timeout.")
    end
end)
t1[9].MouseButton1Click:Connect(function()
    if setclipboard then
        pcall(setclipboard, "https://discord.gg/BR2Vmfbetp")
        t1[9].Text = "Copied!"
    else
        t1[9].Text = "No Clipboard!"
    end

    task.wait(1.5)
    t1[9].Text = "Get Key"
end)
t1[8].MouseButton1Click:Connect(function()
    t1[8].Text = "Checking..."
    local success = pcall(function()
        local Text = t1[7].Text

        if #Text < 5 then
            t1[8].Text = "Too Short!"
            task.wait(1.5)
            t1[8].Text = "Check Key"

            return
        end

        if v6(Text) then
            t1[8].Text = "Correct!"
            task.wait(0.5)
            t1[6]:Destroy()
            t1[5]()

            return
        end

        t1[8].Text = "Wrong Key!"
        task.wait(1.5)
        t1[8].Text = "Check Key"
    end)
    if not success then
        pcall(function()
            t1[6]:Destroy()
        end)
        t1[1]:Kick("[KamScripts] Security violation detected.")
    end
end)
